🧭 Foundation Models / Datasets / BenchmarksFoundation Models / Datasets / Benchmarks

· 发展脉络· Development

该方向致力于解决具身智能从感知到行动的泛化难题，核心演进路径是从单一模态的专用策略向多模态、大参数量的基础模型（Foundation Models）转变。发展初期聚焦于构建标准化的仿真基准与数据集（如Octo, OpenVLA），以解决数据稀缺和评估标准不一的问题。随后，研究重心转向利用世界模型（World Models）和视觉-语言-动作（VLA）架构，通过生成式预训练和物理一致性约束，实现机器人在开放环境中的长程规划与零样本迁移。近期趋势强调物理常识的融入、多模态触觉融合以及从仿真到真实的无缝迁移，旨在构建具备通用物理理解能力的具身大脑。This direction is dedicated to solving the generalization challenge of embodied intelligence from perception to action, with the core evolutionary path shifting from single-modality specialized policies to multi-modal, large-parameter Foundation Models. In the early development stage, the focus was on building standardized simulation benchmarks and datasets (such as Octo, OpenVLA) to address issues of data scarcity and inconsistent evaluation standards. Subsequently, research focus shifted towards leveraging World Models and Visual-Language-Action (VLA) architectures, achieving long-horizon planning and zero-shot transfer for robots in open environments through generative pre-training and physical consistency constraints. Recent trends emphasize the integration of physical commonsense, multi-modal tactile fusion, and seamless Sim-to-Real transfer, aiming to construct an embodied brain with general physical understanding capabilities.

切入角度Perspectives

  • 机器人基础模型架构与训练Robot Foundation Model Architecture and Training
  • 具身智能大规模数据集构建Construction of Large-Scale Datasets for Embodied Intelligence
  • 具身智能基准测试与评估Embodied AI Benchmarking and Evaluation
  • 世界模型与物理仿真World Models and Physical Simulation

· 各角度时间线· Timeline per Perspective

机器人基础模型架构与训练Robot Foundation Model Architecture and Training

该方向聚焦于构建通用机器人基础模型，核心演进从单一模仿学习转向多模态融合与生成式架构。早期工作如Octo和OpenVLA确立了VLA架构的开源基座，解决了数据异构与模型泛化问题。随后，π0与RDT-1B引入Flow Matching与扩散模型，实现了连续动作的高频生成与双臂协同。近期研究如GR-2、π0.5及Magma进一步通过海量视频预训练、混血训练及世界模型技术，强化空间推理与跨域迁移能力。同时，NORA、MolmoAct等致力于轻量化与空间解耦，而RouterVLA与Hallucination研究则关注部署效率与数据质量，整体呈现从“大参数堆叠”向“架构创新、数据工程与物理一致性”并重的趋势。This direction focuses on building general robot foundation models, with a core evolution from single imitation learning towards multi-modal fusion and generative architectures. Early works like Octo and OpenVLA established open-source VLA baselines, addressing data heterogeneity and model generalization. Subsequently, π0 and RDT-1B introduced Flow Matching and diffusion models, achieving high-frequency generation of continuous actions and dual-arm coordination. Recent research such as GR-2, π0.5, and Magma further strengthens spatial reasoning and cross-domain transfer capabilities through massive video pre-training, hybrid training, and world model technologies. Meanwhile, NORA and MolmoAct focus on lightweighting and spatial decoupling, while RouterVLA and Hallucination studies address deployment efficiency and data quality, collectively presenting a trend shifting from "stacking large parameters" to prioritizing "architectural innovation, data engineering, and physical consistency."

2026-09
SolarWM: Open Data and Scalable Training for Long-Horizon Video World Models
动机: Motivation: 解决世界模型训练面临的数据孤岛、异构标准不一以及长视界预测中分布匹配误差累积的问题。Addresses the issues of data silos, inconsistent heterogeneous standards in World Model training, and the accumulation of distribution matching errors in long-horizon prediction.
方法: Method: 开源包含 25TB 数据的统一训练管线，将 10 个异构数据集清洗为 143 万条规范视频片段，并采用因果训练中的分布匹配（DMD）技术。Opens a unified training pipeline containing 25TB of data, cleaning 10 heterogeneous datasets into 1.43 million standardized video clips, and employing Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) technology in causal training.
推进: Advance: 通过大规模数据工程将世界模型训练从“炼丹”转向标准化工程，验证了统一数据标准对长视界视频预测能力的显著提升。Transforms World Model training from 'alchemy' to standardized engineering through large-scale data engineering, verifying the significant improvement in long-horizon video prediction capabilities brought by unified data standards.
2026-09
ShallowStream: Index Shallow then Answer Deep for Streaming Video Understanding
动机: Motivation: 解决流式视频理解中全深度预填充导致的显存爆炸与高延迟问题，优化具身智能实时感知的计算效率。Solves the problem of memory explosion and high latency caused by full-depth pre-filling in streaming video understanding, optimizing the computational efficiency of embodied AI real-time perception.
方法: Method: 提出“浅层索引，深层回答”的流式架构，利用浅层网络进行特征检索，仅在关键帧唤醒全深度模型进行决策。Proposes a streaming architecture of 'shallow indexing, deep answering', utilizing shallow networks for feature retrieval and awakening the full-depth model only at keyframes for decision-making.
推进: Advance: 在保持性能不降的前提下，将计算量降低 52 倍、延迟降低 11 倍，为资源受限的机器人实时视觉处理提供了高效工程方案。Reduces computational load by 52 times and latency by 11 times while maintaining performance, providing an efficient engineering solution for real-time visual processing of resource-constrained robots.
2026-09
Latent Cluster Analysis for Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 针对 VLA 模型潜在空间的黑箱特性，试图通过可解释性分析揭示模型内部动作解码与语义生成的关联机制。Aiming to reveal the internal mechanism linking action decoding and semantic generation in VLA models, this work attempts to analyze the black-box nature of the potential space through interpretability analysis.
方法: Method: 引入基于交叉注意力（cross-attention）的嵌入加权技术，对 GR00T N1.5 模型的潜在空间进行聚类分析以量化语义结构。Introduces embedding weighting technology based on cross-attention to perform clustering analysis on the potential space of the GR00T N1.5 model to quantify semantic structures.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了潜在空间聚类的轮廓分数，初步验证了注意力机制在解耦视觉 - 语言 - 动作特征中的有效性，为模型调试提供新工具。Significantly improves the silhouette score of potential space clustering, preliminarily verifying the effectiveness of attention mechanisms in decoupling Vision-Language-Action features, and providing new tools for model debugging.
2026-09
AGI Maze Prediction Datasets: A Compact Benchmark for Learning World Dynamics with Transformers
动机: Motivation: 剥离复杂的视觉感知干扰，构建一个轻量级基准以纯粹诊断 Transformer 类模型在状态预测与内部记忆维护上的短板。Strips away complex visual perception interference to build a lightweight benchmark that purely diagnoses the shortcomings of Transformer-based models in state prediction and internal memory maintenance.
方法: Method: 设计基于 ASCII 迷宫预测的 AGI 基准，引入伪视频空间记忆（Pseudo-Video Spatial-Memory）机制来模拟隐式状态存储。Designs an AGI benchmark based on ASCII maze prediction, introducing a Pseudo-Video Spatial-Memory mechanism to simulate implicit state storage.
推进: Advance: 证明了显式空间结构记忆比纯字节流更能提升状态预测准确率，揭示了语言接地需持久数据结构支持的本质规律。Demonstrates that explicit spatial structure memory improves state prediction accuracy more than pure byte streams, revealing the essential law that language grounding requires support from persistent data structures.
2026-09
Toward Unified Robot Learning: Bridging Representation, Vision-Language-Action, and World Models
动机: Motivation: 针对机器人学习领域感知、VLA 与世界模型各自为政、缺乏统一理论框架的现状，旨在解决单模块最优但整体泛化能力差的痛点。Aiming to address the pain point of single-module optimality but poor overall generalization capability, this work targets the current status in robot learning where perception, VLA, and World Models operate in isolation without a unified theoretical framework.
方法: Method: 提出了一套涵盖表示学习、VLA 架构与世界模型的分类学 Taxonomy，并构建了理论框架以指导物理接地概率学习的统一路径。Proposes a taxonomy covering representation learning, VLA architectures, and World Models, and constructs a theoretical framework to guide a unified path for physical-grounded probabilistic learning.
推进: Advance: 首次系统梳理了三大核心组件的演进关系，指出了跨本体泛化与 OOD 问题的工程瓶颈，为后续架构融合提供了理论地图。For the first time, systematically reviews the evolutionary relationships of the three core components, identifies engineering bottlenecks in cross-embodiment generalization and Out-of-Distribution (OOD) problems, and provides a theoretical map for subsequent architecture fusion.
2026-09
Semantic Bayesian World Models
动机: Motivation: 批判当前大模型与知识图谱硬拼的架构缺陷，指出概率推理与稳定语义标识符缺失是阻碍超人类智能落地的核心原因。Critiques the architectural defects of the current hard combination of large models and knowledge graphs, pointing out that the lack of probabilistic reasoning and stable semantic identifiers is the core reason hindering the landing of superhuman intelligence.
方法: Method: 提出语义贝叶斯世界模型（SBWM），利用贝叶斯网络将概率大模型与布尔语义知识图谱进行数学层面的统一。Proposes the Semantic Bayesian World Model (SBWM), which mathematically unifies probabilistic large models and Boolean semantic knowledge graphs using Bayesian networks.
推进: Advance: 从理论层面论证了概率必须依附于稳定标识符而非字符串，为神经符号系统在具身智能中的深度融合提供了新范式。Theoretically demonstrates that probability must attach to stable identifiers rather than strings, providing a new paradigm for the deep fusion of neuro-symbolic systems in embodied AI.
2026-09
Dense to MoE Adaptation for Compact Vision Language Action Policies
动机: Motivation: 针对VLA模型参数爆炸导致端侧部署困难的问题，探索在保持性能前提下的模型轻量化路径。Addresses the difficulty of edge-side deployment caused by parameter explosion in VLA models, exploring model lightweighting paths while maintaining performance.
方法: Method: 提出Dense2MoE转换策略，将稠密前馈网络动态适配为混合专家（MoE）架构并动态路由。Proposes a Dense2MoE conversion strategy that dynamically adapts dense feedforward networks into a Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture with dynamic routing.
推进: Advance: 在参数减少40%的情况下仍保持LIBERO基准95%的成功率，为端侧部署提供了可行的架构优化思路。Maintained a 95% success rate on the LIBERO benchmark despite a 40% reduction in parameters, providing a feasible architectural optimization strategy for edge-side deployment.
2026-09
ManiSkillFormer: Demonstration-Free Compositional Manipulation via Task-Conditioned Geometric Contracts
动机: Motivation: 试图摆脱对大量演示数据的依赖，通过几何约束解决长程任务中感知与动作接口模糊的问题。Attempts toeliminate dependence on large amounts of demonstration data by using geometric constraints to resolve the ambiguity between perception and action interfaces in long-horizon tasks.
方法: Method: 构建ManiSkillFormer，利用LLM生成几何合同，结合预定义技能库进行无演示的 compositional 操作。Constructs ManiSkillFormer, utilizing LLMs to generate geometric contracts and combining them with a predefined skill library for demonstration-free compositional manipulation.
推进: Advance: 在长程任务中表现出比纯端到端VLA更高的生存率，但灵活性受限于预定义技能库的覆盖范围。Demonstrated higher survival rates than pure end-to-end VLA models in long-horizon tasks, but flexibility is limited by the coverage of the predefined skill library.
2026-09
StereoPatch: Patch-Aligned RGB-Depth Fusion for Spatial Perception in Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对纯RGB视觉模仿学习中因忽略几何深度信息而导致的接触控制与泛化能力不足问题。Addresses the insufficient contact control and generalization capabilities in pure RGB visual imitation learning caused by the neglect of geometric depth information.
方法: Method: 提出StereoPatch方法，通过交叉注意力机制将深度特征对齐并融合进RGB Patch中。Proposes the StereoPatch method, which aligns and fuses depth features into RGB patches via a cross-attention mechanism.
推进: Advance: 在不修改策略架构的前提下显著提升了空间感知与接触控制精度，验证了多模态融合对提升泛化性的有效性。Significantly improved spatial perception and contact control accuracy without modifying the policy architecture, validating the effectiveness of multimodal fusion in enhancing generalization.
2024-02
Foundation Policies with Hilbert Representations
动机: Motivation: 旨在利用希尔伯特空间的几何性质统一离线强化学习的预训练目标，解决传统方法在复杂动态环境中对确定性假设的依赖及泛化难题。Aims to unify pre-training objectives for offline reinforcement learning by leveraging the geometric properties of Hilbert spaces, addressing the reliance of traditional methods on deterministic assumptions and generalization difficulties in complex dynamic environments.
方法: Method: 提出HILP框架，将状态映射至保持时间距离的隐空间，通过训练策略覆盖所有方向，利用提示向量z实现零样本任务切换。Proposes the HILP framework, which maps states to a latent space preserving temporal distance, trains policies to cover all directions, and enables zero-shot task switching using prompt vectors z.
推进: Advance: 在AntMaze和Kitchen等基准上实现了超越专门设计的GCRL方法的零样本适应能力，为离线预训练提供了新的几何视角。Achieves zero-shot adaptability surpassing specially designed GCRL methods on benchmarks such as AntMaze and Kitchen, offering a new geometric perspective for offline pre-training.
2024-03
Stop Regressing: Training Value Functions via Classification for Scalable Deep RL
动机: Motivation: 针对大模型强化学习中价值函数回归任务因使用MSE损失导致训练不稳定且难以扩展的痛点，指出传统回归范式与Transformer架构不匹配的问题。Addressing the pain points of instability and limited scalability in value function regression tasks within large model reinforcement learning caused by the use of MSE loss, this work identifies the mismatch between traditional regression paradigms and Transformer architectures.
方法: Method: 提出将价值函数训练从回归问题重构为分类问题，利用交叉熵损失替代均方误差损失，使价值评估能够适配大规模Transformer架构。It proposes reconstructing value function training from a regression problem to a classification problem, utilizing cross-entropy loss instead of mean squared error loss, enabling value assessment to adapt to large-scale Transformer architectures.
推进: Advance: 在Atari、机器人控制及棋类博弈中显著提升了训练稳定性和性能，证明了分类范式在价值函数学习中的优越性，为机器人基础模型的强化学习训练提供了新的优化路径。Significantly improving training stability and performance in Atari, robot control, and board games, it demonstrates the superiority of the classification paradigm in value function learning, providing a new optimization path for reinforcement learning training of robot foundation models.
2024-05
Octo: An Open-Source Generalist Robot Policy
动机: Motivation: 打破机器人策略模型对特定传感器和动作空间的强依赖，实现灵活的通用策略。Breaks the strong dependency of robot policy models on specific sensors and action spaces, enabling flexible general policies.
方法: Method: 基于800k多源数据训练Transformer架构的Octo模型，支持多模态输入与动作空间微调。Trains the Transformer-based Octo model on 800k multi-source data, supporting multi-modal inputs and action space fine-tuning.
推进: Advance: 证明了单一模型经少量微调即可适应不同硬件配置，确立了开源通用策略的新基线，但复杂操作泛化仍受限。Demonstrates that a single model can adapt to different hardware configurations with minimal fine-tuning, establishing a new baseline for open-source general policies, though generalization in complex operations remains limited.
2024-06
OpenVLA: An Open-Source Vision-Language-Action Model
动机: Motivation: 挑战大参数即正义的闭源趋势，提供在消费级硬件上可运行的高性价比VLA模型。Challenges the closed-source trend of "bigger parameters are better," providing cost-effective VLA models runnable on consumer-grade hardware.
方法: Method: 缝合Llama 2与视觉编码器，利用LoRA微调技术构建7B参数的OpenVLA模型。Stitches Llama 2 with a visual encoder, utilizing LoRA fine-tuning techniques to construct the 7B-parameter OpenVLA model.
推进: Advance: 以较小参数量超越55B模型性能，大幅降低部署门槛，但受限于单图输入导致推理速度较慢。Outperforms 55B models with a smaller parameter count, significantly lowering deployment barriers, though limited by single-image input leading to slower inference speeds.
2024-10
π0: A Vision-Language-Action Flow Model for General Robot Control
动机: Motivation: 解决VLA动作离散化痛点，利用大模型思维实现高频连续控制与跨形态泛化。Addresses the pain point of VLA action discretization, utilizing large model reasoning to achieve high-frequency continuous control and cross-morphology generalization.
方法: Method: 采用Flow Matching架构，将互联网语义知识与连续动作生成结合，进行跨实体预训练。Adopts a Flow Matching architecture, combining internet semantic knowledge with continuous action generation for cross-entity pre-training.
推进: Advance: 实现了50Hz高频控制，在跨形态任务中展现强泛化能力，但复杂任务仍需大量高质量数据微调。Achieves 50Hz high-frequency control, demonstrating strong generalization in cross-morphology tasks, though complex tasks still require extensive high-quality data fine-tuning.
2024-10
RDT-1B: a Diffusion Foundation Model for Bimanual Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决双臂操作基础模型难题，统一不同机器人的动作空间以支持大规模预训练。Solves the foundational model challenge for dual-arm manipulation, unifying action spaces across different robots to support large-scale pre-training.
方法: Method: 构建1B参数扩散模型，提出物理可解释的统一动作空间，基于1M数据预训练。Constructs a 1B-parameter diffusion model, proposing a physically interpretable unified action space, pre-trained on 1M data.
推进: Advance: 在双臂操作任务上达到SOTA，实现了未见场景的零样本泛化，但训练成本高昂且依赖特定硬件微调。Achieves SOTA on dual-arm manipulation tasks, enabling zero-shot generalization to unseen scenarios, though training costs are high and specific hardware fine-tuning is required.
2024-10
GR-2: A Generative Video-Language-Action Model with Web-Scale Knowledge for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 验证海量网络视频数据对提升机器人操作通用性的价值，引入世界模型辅助规划。Validates the value of massive network video data in enhancing robot manipulation generality, introducing world models to assist planning.
方法: Method: 利用3800万网络视频预训练GR-2，采用生成式视频语言动作模型架构，结合WBC算法兜底。Pre-trains GR-2 using 38 million network videos, adopting a generative video-language-action model architecture, combined with WBC algorithms for fallback.
推进: Advance: 证明了数据规模换性能的可行性，视频生成辅助规划提升了复杂任务成功率，但真实部署仍需传统算法辅助。Proves the feasibility of trading data scale for performance; video generation-assisted planning improves success rates in complex tasks, though real-world deployment still requires traditional algorithm assistance.
2024-12
TraceVLA: Visual Trace Prompting Enhances Spatial-Temporal Awareness for Generalist Robotic Policies
动机: Motivation: 解决VLA模型缺乏长程空间记忆、决策依赖当前帧导致复杂操作失败的问题。Addresses the issue of VLA models lacking long-range spatial memory and decision-making relying on current frames, leading to failures in complex operations.
方法: Method: 引入视觉轨迹提示（Visual Trace Prompting），利用Co-Tracker在图像上绘制历史轨迹。Introduces Visual Trace Prompting, utilizing Co-Tracker to draw historical trajectories on images.
推进: Advance: 以低成本显著提升模型的空间感知与长程任务能力，4B版本在保持性能同时大幅降低推理延迟。Significantly enhances the model's spatial perception and long-horizon task capabilities at low cost; the 4B version maintains performance while drastically reducing inference latency.
2024-12
RLDG: Robotic Generalist Policy Distillation via Reinforcement Learning
动机: Motivation: 利用强化学习生成的高精度动作数据弥补人类演示数据的粗糙与不一致性。Compensates for the roughness and inconsistency of human demonstration data using high-precision action data generated by reinforcement learning.
方法: Method: 设计RL蒸馏框架，用RL策略生成的数据微调机器人基础模型，替代部分人类演示。Designs an RL distillation framework to fine-tune robot foundation models with data generated by RL policies, replacing part of human demonstrations.
推进: Advance: 有效提升了精细操作的一致性，但依赖精心设计的奖励函数，且存在RL策略“抄近道”的风险。Effectively improves consistency in fine manipulation, but relies on carefully designed reward functions and carries the risk of RL policies "cutting corners."
2025-02
RoboBrain: A Unified Brain Model for Robotic Manipulation from Abstract to Concrete
动机: Motivation: 尝试统一机器人从抽象指令理解到具体动作执行的“大脑”，解决多任务割裂问题。Attempts to unify the robot "brain" from abstract instruction understanding to concrete action execution, solving the problem of multi-task fragmentation.
方法: Method: 基于MLLM架构，利用ShareRobot数据集进行多模态微调，整合规划与轨迹预测。Based on the MLLM architecture, utilizes the ShareRobot dataset for multi-modal fine-tuning, integrating planning and trajectory prediction.
推进: Advance: 在RoboVQA等基准上表现优异，展现了多任务统一处理的潜力，但在复杂物理交互与噪声环境下仍有短板。Performs excellently on benchmarks like RoboVQA, demonstrating potential for unified multi-task processing, though still lacking in complex physical interactions and noisy environments.
2025-03
GR00T N1: An Open Foundation Model for Generalist Humanoid Robots
动机: Motivation: 利用海量网络视频与合成数据解决真机数据稀缺问题，构建人形机器人通用基础模型。Utilizes massive network videos and synthetic data to solve the scarcity of real-robot data, building a general foundation model for humanoid robots.
方法: Method: 采用数据金字塔策略与双系统架构（System 2推理+System 1动作），模拟人类认知过程。Adopts a data pyramid strategy and a dual-system architecture (System 2 reasoning + System 1 action), simulating human cognitive processes.
推进: Advance: 实现了仿真到真实的高性能迁移，验证了数据规模对泛化的关键作用，但计算资源门槛极高。Achieves high-performance Sim-to-Real transfer, validating the critical role of data scale in generalization, though the computational resource threshold is extremely high.
2025-04
π0.5: a Vision-Language-Action Model with Open-World Generalization
动机: Motivation: 解决VLA模型在未见环境下的泛化难题，通过跨实体知识蒸馏实现长程任务执行。Addresses the generalization challenge of VLA models in unseen environments, achieving long-horizon task execution through cross-entity knowledge distillation.
方法: Method: 采用“混血训练”策略，利用97.6%的非目标机器人数据进行预训练，结合分层推理。Adopts a "hybrid training" strategy, pre-training on 97.6% non-target robot data, combined with hierarchical reasoning.

Human-Centric Transferable Tactile Pre-Training for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对机器人触觉数据稀缺且难以与视觉模态对齐的瓶颈，旨在利用人类触觉数据构建通用预训练框架以解决精细操作中的感知鸿沟。Aiming to address the bottleneck of scarce robot tactile data and its difficulty in aligning with the visual modality, this work seeks to utilize human tactile data to build a general pre-training framework to bridge the perception gap in fine manipulation.
方法: Method: 提出TTP框架构建H-Tac大规模触觉数据集，将触觉模态显式融入VLA架构并通过统一空间实现人机触觉对齐。Proposes the TTP framework to construct the large-scale H-Tac tactile dataset, explicitly integrating the tactile modality into the VLA architecture and achieving human-robot tactile alignment through a unified space.
推进: Advance: 证明了触觉预训练在精细操作任务上的显著价值，实现了跨形态的人机触觉对齐，但受限于触觉硬件离散性，尚未完全消除分布偏移。Demonstrates the significant value of tactile pre-training in fine manipulation tasks, achieving cross-embodiment human-robot tactile alignment, but distribution shift is not yet fully eliminated due to the discreteness of tactile hardware.

Lift3D-VLA: Lifting VLA Models to 3D Geometry and Dynamics-Aware Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 现有VLA模型多基于2D图像，缺乏对3D几何与物理动态的感知，难以处理需要深度理解的空间操作任务。Existing VLA models are mostly based on 2D images, lacking perception of 3D geometry and physical dynamics, making it difficult to handle spatial manipulation tasks requiring deep understanding.
方法: Method: 提出Lift3D-VLA，将点云投影到虚拟平面复用2D编码，利用GC-MAE进行“现在重建+未来预测”的双目标训练，增强几何与动态感知。Proposes Lift3D-VLA, projecting point clouds onto virtual planes to reuse 2D encoding, utilizing GC-MAE for dual-objective training of 'present reconstruction + future prediction' to enhance geometric and dynamic perception.
推进: Advance: 在缺乏大规模3D数据的情况下，通过巧妙的2D提升策略实现了3D几何感知，证明了层级动作建模能提升长程规划的连贯性。Achieves 3D geometric perception through a clever 2D lifting strategy despite the lack of large-scale 3D data, proving that hierarchical action modeling can improve the coherence of long-horizon planning.

From Foundation to Application: Improving VLA Models in Practice
动机: Motivation: VLA模型在实验室Benchmark上表现优异，但在真实复杂动态环境中泛化性差、延迟高，难以从理论走向实际应用。VLA models perform excellently on laboratory benchmarks but exhibit poor generalization, high latency, and difficulty in transitioning from theory to practical application in real-world complex dynamic environments.
方法: Method: 提出LingBot-VLA 2.0，通过6万小时多配置数据蒸馏与MoE架构优化，结合双查询蒸馏技术提升模型在实践中的鲁棒性。We propose LingBot-VLA 2.0, which enhances model robustness in practice through 60,000 hours of multi-configuration data distillation, MoE architecture optimization, and dual-query distillation techniques.
推进: Advance: 在GM-100及移动操作任务上验证了大规模数据与蒸馏策略的有效性，证明了从基础模型到应用落地的关键路径在于数据规模与架构效率的平衡。We validated the effectiveness of large-scale data and distillation strategies on GM-100 and mobile manipulation tasks, demonstrating that the key path from foundation models to practical deployment lies in balancing data scale and architectural efficiency.

动机: Motivation: 针对当前世界动作模型与VLA研究因接口不统一和语义隐藏导致经验无法积累、陷入单纯堆叠参数的困境。Addressing the current dilemma where world action models and VLA research suffer from accumulated experience failure andfall into a trap of merely stacking parameters due to inconsistent interfaces and hidden semantics.
方法: Method: 提出“具身大脑”架构蓝图，将推理与执行解耦，并呼吁社区建立标准化的接口“合同”以统一语义。Proposing an 'Embodied Brain' architectural blueprint that decouples reasoning from execution, and calling for the community to establish standardized interface 'contracts' to unify semantics.
推进: Advance: 从理论层面厘清了预测与执行的分离必要性，指出了当前架构在跨域迁移中因缺乏标准导致的根本矛盾。Clarifying from a theoretical perspective the necessity of separating prediction from execution, and pointing out the fundamental contradiction in current architectures regarding cross-domain transfer due to the lack of standards.

Artificial Foveated Perception for Mitigating Shortcut Learning in Robotic Foundation Models
动机: Motivation: 针对机器人基础模型在微调过程中容易依赖背景捷径（Shortcut Learning）而非关注关键物体，导致长程任务泛化差的问题。Addressing the problem where robot foundation models tend to rely on background shortcuts (Shortcut Learning) rather than focusing on key objects during fine-tuning, leading to poor generalization in long-horizon tasks.
方法: Method: 引入人工黄斑感知（AFP）机制，在微调阶段通过辅助损失强制引导注意力聚焦物体，推理阶段移除该模块以零成本部署。Introducing an Artificial Foveal Perception (AFP) mechanism that forces attention to focus on objects via auxiliary loss during the fine-tuning phase, while removing this module during the inference phase for zero-cost deployment.
推进: Advance: 提供了一种轻量级的数据增强策略，显著提升了模型在分布外（OOD）场景下的长程任务成功率，解决了VLA微调中的注意力偏差问题。Providing a lightweight data augmentation strategy that significantly improves the success rate of long-horizon tasks in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios, solving the attention bias problem in VLA fine-tuning.

MARS-RA: Rank Aggregation for Credit Assignment via Multimodal Comparisons in Embodied Multi-Agent Cooperation
动机: Motivation: 在具身强化学习中，直接量化单步动作的绝对贡献（信用分配）极其困难，且依赖人工标注或复杂理论计算，难以适应多模态基础模型环境。In embodied reinforcement learning, directly quantifying the absolute contribution of single-step actions (credit assignment) is extremely difficult, relying on manual annotation or complex theoretical calculations, making it hard to adapt to multimodal foundation model environments.
方法: Method: 提出MARS-RA框架，利用多模态大模型（LMM）作为裁判，将信用分配问题转化为基于多模态比较的排名聚合任务，并引入Shapley值作为理论参考。Proposes the MARS-RA framework, utilizing Multimodal Large Models (LMMs) as judges to transform the credit assignment problem into a ranking aggregation task based on multimodal comparison, and introduces Shapley values as a theoretical reference.
推进: Advance: 成功将LMM的视觉理解能力转化为策略更新的奖励信号，在仿真与真实机器人上证明了基于相对排序的信用分配优于传统QMIX方法，为黑盒策略提供了可解释性。Successfully converts the visual understanding capabilities of LMMs into reward signals for strategy updates, demonstrating that relative ranking-based credit assignment outperforms traditional QMIX methods on both simulation and real robots, providing interpretability for black-box strategies.

动机: Motivation: 具身智能体在执行长程任务时，单步错误极易导致后续连锁失败，而现有的事后反思机制往往滞后且效率低下。When embodied agents execute long-horizon tasks, single-step errors easily lead to subsequent chain failures, while existing post-hoc reflection mechanisms are often lagging and inefficient.
方法: Method: 构建轨迹图（Trajectory Graphs）并利用图神经网络（GNN）在任务执行前进行错误诊断，通过图结构捕捉状态转移中的异常模式。Constructs Trajectory Graphs and utilizes Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) for error diagnosis before task execution, capturing anomalous patterns in state transitions through graph structures.
推进: Advance: 实现了从“事后纠错”到“事前预防”的范式转变，证明了基于图结构的诊断能显著降低贝叶斯风险，尽管当前实验主要基于文本环境，但为具身场景提供了新的错误检测架构。Achieves a paradigm shift from 'post-hoc error correction' to 'pre-hoc prevention', proving that graph-structure-based diagnosis significantly reduces Bayesian risk; although current experiments are primarily based on text environments, it provides a new error detection architecture for embodied scenarios.

The SIGReg Objective as Variational Free Energy: A Theoretical Active-Inference Account of JEPA World Models
动机: Motivation: 指出当前JEPA类世界模型在理论推导上存在缺陷，未能正确计算变分自由能，导致“虚假繁荣”和规划误差累积。Points out theoretical flaws in current JEPA-style world models, specifically the incorrect calculation of variational free energy, leading to "false prosperity" and the accumulation of planning errors.
方法: Method: 通过Lean 4机器验证证明SIGReg目标函数是唯一能将JEPA转化为合法Active Inference变分自由能的方法，强制各向同性高斯分布。Uses Lean 4 machine verification to prove that the SIGReg objective function is the unique method to transform JEPA into a valid Active Inference variational free energy, enforcing isotropic Gaussian distributions.
推进: Advance: 建立了精确的理论对应关系，揭示了多步规划中的误差累积机制，为自监督学习提供了坚实的理论地基。Establishes a precise theoretical correspondence, revealing the error accumulation mechanism in multi-step planning and providing a solid theoretical foundation for self-supervised learning.

FlowWAM: Optical Flow as a Unified Action Representation for World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 试图解决世界动作模型（WAM）中动作与视觉模态的鸿沟，利用视频生成模型天然适配光流特性的优势统一动作表示。Attempts to bridge the gap between action and visual modalities in World Action Models (WAM), leveraging the natural adaptation of video generation models to optical flow characteristics to unify action representation.
方法: Method: 提出FlowWAM，将光流转换为HSV图直接输入，采用双流架构共享Backbone，省去传统Tokenizer。Proposes FlowWAM, which converts optical flow into HSV maps for direct input, adopts a dual-stream architecture sharing a Backbone, and eliminates the traditional Tokenizer.
推进: Advance: 在RoboTwin和WorldArena上验证了光流作为统一动作表示的有效性，有效降低了模态转换成本，但牺牲了部分语义信息。Validates the effectiveness of optical flow as a unified action representation on RoboTwin and WorldArena, effectively reducing modality conversion costs at the expense of some semantic information.

动机: Motivation: 针对现有视觉语言模型在空间推理任务中，直接蒸馏几何特征会破坏语言对齐能力并导致物体跟踪崩塌的问题。Addressing the issue that directly distilling geometric features in existing Vision-Language Models for spatial reasoning tasks destroys language alignment capabilities and leads to object tracking collapse.

推进: Advance: 在保持参数量的同时显著提升了空间推理能力，证明了“关系蒸馏”比“特征蒸馏”更能有效平衡语言理解与几何感知。Significantly enhancing spatial reasoning capabilities while maintaining parameter counts, demonstrating that 'relation distillation' is more effective than 'feature distillation' in balancing language understanding and geometric perception.

具身智能大规模数据集构建Construction of Large-Scale Datasets for Embodied Intelligence

具身智能大规模数据集构建正从单一模态向多模态融合、从封闭仿真向开放真实世界演进。早期工作聚焦于遥操作轨迹采集（如DROID），解决数据稀缺问题；随后引入反事实推理（OmniDrive）与触觉对齐（T3, RCT），提升模型对物理规律与社会交互的理解。当前趋势转向构建“世界模型”底座（Cosmos, PhysEditWorld），通过海量视频合成与物理规则显式化，弥合Sim-to-Real鸿沟。同时，数据标准（Data Standards）与人类交互维度（HABIT）的引入，标志着数据集正从单纯的性能提升工具，转向支撑具身智能社会兼容性与物理常识推理的基础设施。The construction of large-scale datasets for embodied intelligence is evolving from single-modality to multi-modal fusion, and from closed simulation to open real-world environments. Early work focused on teleoperation trajectory collection (e.g., DROID) to address data scarcity; subsequently, counterfactual reasoning (OmniDrive) and tactile alignment (T3, RCT) were introduced to enhance models' understanding of physical laws and social interactions. Current trends shift towards building "world model" foundations (Cosmos, PhysEditWorld), bridging the Sim-to-Real gap through massive video synthesis and explicit physical rules. Meanwhile, the introduction of data standards (Data Standards) and human interaction dimensions (HABIT) marks a transition of datasets from mere performance enhancement tools to infrastructure supporting the social compatibility and physical commonsense reasoning of embodied intelligence.

2026-09

推进: Advance: 通过强制视觉优先范式成功抑制了模型对文本的过度依赖，证明了工具使用意愿与视觉理解能力比单纯堆砌参数更能提升深度推理性能，为视频流代理训练提供了新范式。By enforcing a vision-first paradigm, we successfully suppress the model's over-reliance on text, proving that tool usage willingness and visual understanding capabilities enhance deep reasoning performance more effectively than simply stacking parameters, providing a new paradigm for video stream agent training.

推进: Advance: 证明了在跨域场景下，潜在预测比像素重建更适合动态建模，显著提升了κ值，验证了冻结编码器作为测量工具在特定领域的潜力。It is proven that in cross-domain scenarios, latent prediction is more suitable for dynamic modeling than pixel reconstruction, significantly improving the κ value and validating the potential of frozen encoders as measurement tools in specific domains.

方法: Method: 构建了包含100个多样化任务的DexVerse基准，整合多机械臂形态与多视觉变体，并利用VR遥操作采集高保真接触力数据。Constructing the DexVerse benchmark containing 100 diverse tasks, integrating multiple manipulator morphologies and visual variants, and utilizing VR teleoperation to collect high-fidelity contact force data.
推进: Advance: 实验揭示VLA预训练在灵巧操作上并未优于扩散策略，且暴露了现有模型在亚毫米级对齐与力控上的巨大差距，证明了当前SOTA离通用灵巧操作仍有十万八千里。Experiments reveal that VLA pre-training does not outperform diffusion policies in dexterous manipulation, exposing a huge gap in sub-millimeter alignment and force control in existing models, proving that current SOTA is still far from general dexterous manipulation.

Do Egocentric Video-Language Models Capture Both Hand- and Object-Centric Cues?
动机: Motivation: 揭露现有自监督视频语言模型过度依赖背景或静态物体位置等捷径线索，缺乏对真实手物交互动态的鲁棒理解。Revealing that existing self-supervised video-language models overly rely on shortcut cues such as background or static object positions, lacking robust understanding of real hand-object interaction dynamics.
方法: Method: 提出CI-HOI评估框架，通过外部检测器人为遮挡关键的手部或物体信息，强制模型在缺失核心视觉线索下预测动作。Proposing the CI-HOI evaluation framework, which artificially occludes key hand or object information via external detectors, forcing the model to predict actions under the absence of core visual cues.
推进: Advance: 证实了模型在常规测试中的高准确率多为“作弊”结果，并验证了其在机器人视角下的泛化能力，表明真正的动态交互特征学习仍极具挑战。Confirming that high accuracy in conventional tests is often a result of 'cheating', and validating its generalization capability from a robot's perspective, indicating that learning true dynamic interaction features remains highly challenging.

DA-Nav: Direction-Aware City-Scale Vision-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决具身智能在长程导航中依赖昂贵SLAM地图及稀疏指令下泛化能力不足的问题，探索无需精细地图的粗粒度城市级导航方案。Aiming to solve the problem of insufficient generalization capability of embodied AI in long-horizon navigation due to reliance on expensive SLAM maps and sparse instructions, exploring coarse-grained city-level navigation solutions without fine maps.
方法: Method: 提出DA-Nav框架，将连续路径回归转化为图像平面上的离散网格定位，结合思维链（CoT）进行轨迹恢复，并构建ReDA数据集以补充方向感知数据。Proposing the DA-Nav framework, which transforms continuous path regression into discrete grid localization on the image plane, combining Chain-of-Thought (CoT) for trajectory recovery, and constructing the ReDA dataset to supplement direction-aware data.
推进: Advance: 实现了在CARLA仿真及真机环境下的跨平台迁移与长程闭环控制，证明了VLM在稀疏指令下具备鲁棒导航潜力，填补了方向感知数据的空白。Achieving cross-platform transfer and long-horizon closed-loop control in CARLA simulation and real-world environments, proving the robust navigation potential of VLMs under sparse instructions and filling the gap in direction-aware data.

3D-DefectBench: A Controlled Factorial Study of Vision-Language Model Evaluation Pipelines for Fine-Grained 3D Generation Defects
动机: Motivation: 针对当前VLM作为3D生成质量裁判时缺乏系统性评估、且过度依赖高成本渲染方案导致资源浪费的现状，亟需建立可配置、成本感知的评测体系。Addressing the current status where VLMs lack systematic evaluation as 3D generation quality judges and cause resource waste due to over-reliance on high-cost rendering schemes, necessitating the establishment of a configurable, cost-aware evaluation system.
方法: Method: 构建3D-DefectBench基准，通过控制变量的因子实验（Factorial Study）系统评估不同输入模态（如RGB、深度图）及渲染配置对VLM评估性能的影响。Constructing the 3D-DefectBench benchmark, systematically evaluating the impact of different input modalities (e.g., RGB, depth maps) and rendering configurations on VLM evaluation performance through controlled variable factorial studies.
推进: Advance: 发现低成本6视角RGB方案即可达到最佳评估效果，推翻了深度图等高成本模态的必要性，并揭示了VLM在纹理缺陷评估上的噪声问题，确立了评估配置本身对结果的关键影响。Discovering that a low-cost 6-view RGB scheme can achieve the best evaluation results, overturning the necessity of high-cost modalities like depth maps, and revealing noise issues in VLMs for texture defect evaluation, establishing the critical impact of evaluation configuration itself on results.

方法: Method: 构建包含143万样本的UESF-Bench数据集，引入干扰项并联合训练SeekFollow-VLA模型，利用任务驱动的路由器机制动态决策搜索或跟随策略。The UESF-Bench dataset containing 1.43 million samples is constructed, introducing distractors and jointly training the SeekFollow-VLA model, utilizing a task-driven router mechanism to dynamically decide between search or follow strategies.
推进: Advance: 首次将“找人”从被动跟随转变为主动搜索，揭示了多人在场场景下模型成功率显著下降的泛化瓶颈，为具身智能的长程感知能力提供了新标尺。For the first time, "finding people" is transformed from passive following to active search, revealing a generalization bottleneck where model success rates significantly drop in scenarios with multiple people present, providing a new benchmark for the long-range perceptual capabilities of embodied intelligence.

推进: Advance: 重新定义了视频生成作为验证因果思维的媒介，指出闭源模型在物理规律内化上的不足，打破了“视频生成即世界模型”的盲目叙事。Video generation is redefined as a medium for verifying causal thinking, pointing out the insufficiency of closed-source models in internalizing physical laws, and breaking the blind narrative that "video generation equals world models."

方法: Method: 提出FDR和FDH指标量化因素偏差，设计Bias-Aware评估策略以识别颜色、动词等特征对模型决策的权重影响。The FDR and FDH metrics are proposed to quantify factor bias, and a Bias-Aware evaluation strategy is designed to identify the weight impact of features such as color and verbs on model decisions.
推进: Advance: 证实颜色特征在桌面操作中具有过强的主导性，并证明基于偏差感知的数据收集策略可节省50%演示数据，为高效训练提供了新范式。It is confirmed that color features have an overly dominant influence in tabletop operations, and it is proven that bias-aware data collection strategies can save 50% of demonstration data, providing a new paradigm for efficient training.

动机: Motivation: 针对现有长程导航基准过度依赖语言指令和全局信号，无法评估模型在无辅助下的长视野证据整合与自主规划能力。Addressing the issue that existing long-range navigation benchmarks overly rely on language instructions and global signals, failing to evaluate the model's ability to integrate long-range evidence and plan autonomously without assistance.

动机: Motivation: 针对开放世界视频异常检测（OWVAD）模型普遍存在的“定义盲视”现象，即模型无法根据用户指定的异常定义调整检测策略。Addressing the prevalent "definition blindness" in open-world video anomaly detection (OWVAD) models, where models cannot adjust detection strategies based on user-specified anomaly definitions.
方法: Method: 提出DeCoS方法，通过对比学习强制模型区分不同定义间的证据，并重新定义评估指标以排除正常帧主导的干扰。The DeCoS method is proposed, using contrastive learning to force models to distinguish evidence between different definitions, and redefining evaluation metrics to exclude interference dominated by normal frames.
推进: Advance: 揭示了现有高指标背后的虚假繁荣，证明模型往往忽略指令而仅检测通用异常，推动了OWVAD从单纯定位向定义条件响应的评估转变。This work reveals the false prosperity behind existing high metrics, proving that models often ignore instructions and only detect generic anomalies, promoting the shift of OWVAD evaluation from simple localization to definition-conditioned response.

动机: Motivation: 针对现有具身世界模型评测依赖逆动力学模型（IDM）黑盒导致归因模糊（无法区分生成错误还是动作提取错误）的问题。Addressing the problem of ambiguous attribution in existing embodied world model evaluations that rely on the black-box inverse dynamics model (IDM), making it impossible to distinguish between generation errors and action extraction errors.
方法: Method: 提出KineBench，摒弃IDM黑盒，采用显式的3D运动学接地，串联YOLO、MoGe等感知模块构建透明评测管道。KineBench is proposed, abandoning the IDM black box, adopting explicit 3D kinematic grounding, and constructing a transparent evaluation pipeline by chaining perception modules such as YOLO and MoGe.
推进: Advance: 实现了从像素空间到运动学空间的评测范式转移，显著提升了物理可行性评估的可解释性，并发现数据量堆叠无法解决复杂物理交互的“复杂度限制”现象。A paradigm shift in evaluation from pixel space to kinematic space is achieved, significantly improving the interpretability of physical feasibility assessment, and discovering that data stacking cannot solve the "complexity limitation" phenomenon in complex physical interactions.

SpatialCLI: Learning to Reason With Spatial Tools, Then Without Them
动机: Motivation: 解决VLM在空间推理任务中“眼高手低”的痛点，探索通过工具辅助到参数内化的路径以提升空间理解能力。Address the pain point of VLMs exhibiting "high aspirations but low capabilities" in spatial reasoning tasks, exploring a path from tool assistance to parameter internalization to enhance spatial understanding.

推进: Advance: 证实开源模型在运动理解上接近随机猜测，指出单纯增加帧数无法解决模型未学会看运动的根本缺陷。It is confirmed that open-source models are close to random guessing in motion understanding, pointing out that simply increasing frame counts cannot solve the fundamental defect that models have not learned to perceive motion.

动机: Motivation: 旨在解决具身智能在主动视觉任务中难以应对意外遮挡、导致“瞎子摸象”式泛化失效的核心痛点。Aiming to solve the core pain point of embodied intelligence in active vision tasks struggling to cope with unexpected occlusions, leading to 'blind men touching an elephant' style generalization failure.

推进: Advance: 将“看不见”从边缘问题提升为核心评测任务，通过量化指标（CVG）迫使策略学会真正的视觉推理而非死记硬背。Elevated 'invisibility' from an edge issue to a core evaluation task, forcing strategies to learn true visual reasoning rather than rote memorization through quantitative metrics (CVG).

世界模型与物理仿真World Models and Physical Simulation

该方向致力于构建具备物理一致性与因果推理能力的具身世界模型，核心思路是从纯视觉生成转向“物理感知 + 生成先验”的融合。早期工作如InFusion和Neural Gaffer利用扩散模型隐式学习光照与几何，解决了传统渲染效率低的问题，但缺乏显式物理约束。随后，ManiSkill3和RoboTwin等基准与仿真平台通过GPU并行化与生成式数字孪生，降低了大规模策略训练的数据成本。最新演进（如PhysCtrl、A2World、RoboWorld）强调将动作作为条件输入，利用神经符号架构或物理约束损失函数，使模型不仅能预测像素，还能理解物体动力学与交互逻辑，从而在Sim-to-Real迁移中实现更鲁棒的泛化。This direction is dedicated to building embodied world models with physical consistency and causal reasoning capabilities, with the core idea shifting from pure visual generation to a fusion of 'physical perception + generative priors'. Early works like InFusion and Neural Gaffer utilized diffusion models to implicitly learn lighting and geometry, solving the low efficiency of traditional rendering, but lacked explicit physical constraints. Subsequently, benchmarks and simulation platforms such as ManiSkill3 and RoboTwin reduced data costs for large-scale policy training through GPU parallelization and generative digital twins. The latest evolution (e.g., PhysCtrl, A2World, RoboWorld) emphasizes treating actions as conditional inputs, leveraging neural-symbolic architectures or physical constraint loss functions, enabling models not only to predict pixels but also to understand object dynamics and interaction logic, thereby achieving more robust generalization in Sim-to-Real transfer.

Toward Unified Robot Learning: Bridging Representation, Vision-Language-Action, and World Models
动机: Motivation: 针对当前机器人学习中感知、VLA 与 world models 各自为政、缺乏统一物理基础的混乱现状，旨在构建一个涵盖表示学习与因果推理的“大统一”理论框架。Addresses the chaotic status quo where perception, VLA, and world models in current robot learning operate in silos without a unified physical foundation, aiming to construct a 'Grand Unified' theoretical framework encompassing representation learning and causal reasoning.
方法: Method: 提出了一套分类学 Taxonomy，将现有方法按轴分类，并倡导从“单模块最优”转向“物理接地（Physical Grounded）的概率性学习”范式。Proposes a Taxonomy to classify existing methods along axes, advocating a paradigm shift from 'single-module optimization' to 'physically grounded probabilistic learning'.
推进: Advance: 首次系统性地梳理了世界模型与具身智能的融合路径，指出了跨本体泛化与 OOD 问题的核心症结，为后续研究提供了理论地图而非具体代码。Systematically maps the integration paths between world models and embodied intelligence for the first time, identifying the core issues of cross-embodiment generalization and OOD problems, providing a theoretical map rather than specific code for future research.

Semantic Bayesian World Models
动机: Motivation: 揭示当前架构中知识图谱（布尔逻辑）与大模型（概率分布）的割裂，试图解决 AI 缺乏稳定语义标识与不确定性建模能力的致命伤。Reveals the disconnect between knowledge graphs (Boolean logic) and large models (probability distributions) in current architectures, attempting to solve the fatal flaw of AI lacking stable semantic identifiers and uncertainty modeling capabilities.
方法: Method: 提出语义贝叶斯世界模型（SBWM），利用贝叶斯网络将语义知识与概率推理统一，主张概率必须依附于稳定标识符而非字符串。Proposes the Semantic Bayesian World Model (SBWM), using Bayesian networks to unify semantic knowledge with probabilistic reasoning, asserting that probability must be attached to stable identifiers rather than strings.
推进: Advance: 从理论层面论证了神经符号架构在具身智能中的必要性，为未来实现超人类智能提供了“概率依附于稳定标识”的新范式，尽管工程落地尚远。Theoretically argues for the necessity of neuro-symbolic architectures in embodied intelligence, providing a new paradigm of 'probability attached to stable identifiers' for achieving superhuman intelligence in the future, although engineering implementation is still distant.

Building Pretraining Data for World Models: An Unreal Engine-Based Pipeline for Action-Conditioned Video Generation
动机: Motivation: 解决世界模型训练数据匮乏且质量参差不齐的问题，试图通过大规模高保真仿真数据来支撑长视界视频生成与物理预测。Addresses the scarcity and uneven quality of training data for world models, attempting to support long-horizon video generation and physical prediction through large-scale high-fidelity simulation data.
方法: Method: 构建基于 Unreal Engine 的数据生成流水线，利用 25 台高性能 GPU 集群，通过两阶段渲染（物理模拟+图像生成）产出 9000 小时视频数据。Constructs a data generation pipeline based on Unreal Engine, utilizing a cluster of 25 high-performance GPUs to produce 9,000 hours of video data through a two-stage rendering process (physical simulation + image generation).
推进: Advance: 证明了算力堆砌与工程化数据流水线在填补数据缺口上的有效性，但同时也暴露了离线渲染与实时仿真割裂的局限性，为数据驱动的世界模型树立了新基准。Demonstrates the effectiveness of computational stacking and engineering data pipelines in filling data gaps, while also exposing the limitations of the disconnect between offline rendering and real-time simulation, setting a new benchmark for data-driven world models.

动机: Motivation: 针对预训练世界模型在具身控制中面临的后门攻击风险，揭示仅关注准确率而忽视潜在动力学安全性的隐患。Regarding the backdoor attack risks faced by pre-trained world models in embodied control, reveals the hidden dangers of focusing solely on accuracy while neglecting potential dynamic safety.

推进: Advance: 发现世界模型若缺乏显式物理约束，其潜在动力学极易被恶意重塑，警示构建具身世界模型必须将安全性与物理一致性纳入核心设计考量。Finds that if world models lack explicit physical constraints, their latent dynamics are easily maliciously reshaped, warning that building embodied world models must incorporate safety and physical consistency into core design considerations.

动机: Motivation: 旨在解决现有世界模型缺乏显式物理约束、难以从纯视觉数据中归纳真实动力学规律的问题，试图通过离散符号系统解耦物理动态与视觉外观。Aims to address the lack of explicit physical constraints in existing world models and the difficulty in inducing real dynamic laws from pure visual data, attempting to decouple physical dynamics from visual appearance through a discrete symbolic system.
方法: Method: 提出“物理语言”概念，构建Reason-then-Render架构，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）的常识推理生成离散物理状态，再驱动渲染模块生成下一帧。Proposes the concept of 'Physical Language', constructing a Reason-then-Render architecture that utilizes the commonsense reasoning of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to generate discrete physical states, which then drive the rendering module to generate the next frame.
推进: Advance: 实现了零样本运动迁移，证明了离散物理语言作为通用接口比纯像素预测更具可控性，但揭示了其依赖VLM归纳而非真实物理引擎的局限性。Achieves zero-shot motion transfer, proving that discrete physical language as a universal interface is more controllable than pure pixel prediction, but reveals its limitations in relying on VLM induction rather than real physical engines.

方法: Method: 提出Cycle-World架构，引入反向预测模型构建时间可逆循环，通过正向与反向预测的一致性约束来抑制长序列生成的累积误差。Propose the Cycle-World architecture, introducing a reverse prediction model to construct a time-reversible cycle, suppressing cumulative errors in long-sequence generation through consistency constraints between forward and reverse predictions.

动机: Motivation: 解决现有视频扩散模型在模拟物理动态时因强行负载均衡导致物体在时间轴上运动逻辑断裂（“精神分裂”）的问题。Solving the problem of existing video diffusion models causing motion logic breaks on the time axis (schizophrenia) for objects when simulating physical dynamics due to forced load balancing.
方法: Method: 设计SplitMoE架构，将专家网络显式拆分为“语义”与“通用”双分支，利用冻结VAE特征引导，分别处理物理场景的宏观结构与微观细节。Designed the SplitMoE architecture, explicitly splitting expert networks into 'semantic' and 'general' dual branches, utilizing frozen VAE features for guidance to handle the macro structure and micro details of physical scenes respectively.
推进: Advance: 通过解耦专家角色显著提升了视频生成在时间维度上的物理连贯性与收敛速度，但推理时的路由开销仍是大规模物理仿真部署的瓶颈。Significantly improved the physical coherence and convergence speed of video generation in the time dimension by decoupling expert roles, but the routing overhead during inference remains a bottleneck for large-scale physical simulation deployment.
2026-09
HelixWorld: A Real-time Interactive Audio-Visual World Model
动机: Motivation: 填补具身世界模型在物理交互中缺乏听觉反馈与空间声学一致性的短板，以增强多模态环境感知的闭环控制能力。Filling the shortcoming of embodied world models lacking auditory feedback and spatial acoustic consistency in physical interactions, to enhance closed-loop control capabilities of multi-modal environmental perception.
方法: Method: 构建HelixWorld实时交互模型，通过在线轨迹蒸馏技术同步生成视频与空间音频，并清洗大规模数据以训练声学衰减与接触声模型。Constructed the HelixWorld real-time interaction model, synchronously generating video and spatial audio through online trajectory distillation technology, and cleaning large-scale data to train acoustic attenuation and contact sound models.
推进: Advance: 首次实现了音视频同步的实时具身世界模型生成，验证了听觉反馈对物理交互的补充价值，但在复杂物理接触声的泛化上仍需验证。First achieved real-time embodied world model generation with synchronized audio and video, verifying the supplementary value of auditory feedback for physical interaction, but generalization on complex physical contact sounds still needs verification.
2026-09
Generative Interactions: Weaving Multiparty Human Motion with Bilevel Latent Dynamics
动机: Motivation: 针对多主体具身智能在复杂社交物理环境中运动轨迹难以解耦与预测的问题，试图将群体动力学与个体风格分离。Addressing the challenge of decoupling and predicting motion trajectories of multi-agent embodied intelligence in complex social-physical environments, this work attempts to separate group dynamics from individual styles.
方法: Method: 提出Generative Interactions方法，利用双层潜变量动态模型将多主体运动解耦为“群体节奏”与“个人风格”，并引入元迁移机制处理部分观测。We propose the Generative Interactions method, which utilizes a two-layer latent variable dynamic model to decouple multi-agent motion into 'group rhythm' and 'personal style', and introduces a meta-transfer mechanism to handle partial observations.
推进: Advance: 构建了可解释的社交物理状态接口，提升了多智能体在碎片化数据下的运动预测能力，但长序列生成的误差累积问题尚未完全解决。An interpretable social-physical state interface is constructed, enhancing motion prediction capabilities for multi-agent systems under fragmented data, although the error accumulation issue in long-sequence generation remains not fully resolved.
2026-09
V-JEPA Policy: Building Effective World-Action Models on Predictive Visual Latents
动机: Motivation: 质疑当前具身世界模型过度依赖大规模视频生成参数，探索仅通过预测性潜空间（而非像素生成）实现高效物理交互策略的可能性。We question the current over-reliance of embodied world models on large-scale video generation parameters and explore the possibility of achieving efficient physical interaction strategies solely through predictive latent spaces (rather than pixel generation).
方法: Method: 提出V-JEPA Policy，利用冻结的V-JEPA编码器构建预测性潜空间，直接训练动作专家进行未来状态预测，摒弃了显式的视频生成器。We propose V-JEPA Policy, which utilizes a frozen V-JEPA encoder to construct a predictive latent space, directly training action experts for future state prediction, thereby discarding explicit video generators.
推进: Advance: 证明了在极低参数量下，基于预测性表征的世界模型在Sim-to-Real迁移中优于传统生成式VLA，确立了“预测即控制”在具身物理任务中的核心地位。We demonstrate that world models based on predictive representations outperform traditional generative VLA in Sim-to-Real transfer even with extremely low parameter counts, establishing the core status of 'prediction as control' in embodied physical tasks.
